A1 is an account/title string (NOT the sheet name).\n      - Row 5 are column headers.\n      - Vendor line items start at row 6.\n      - Control rows follow immediately after line items:\n          Month Totals, Ending Balance, Variance, GL Balance\n      - Column O (15) is the Total column used by the Summary sheet.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Title\n    ws[\"A1\"] = title_a1\n\n    # Headers\n    headers = [\"Vendor\", \"Beginning Balance\"] + [m_label for _, m_label in MONTHS] + [\"Total\"]\n    header_row = 5\n    for col_idx, h in enumerate(headers, start=1):\n        ws.cell(row=header_row, column=col_idx, value=h)\n\n    # Vendor rows\n    first_data_row = header_row + 1\n    df = df.copy()\n\n    # Normalize expected columns\n    required = [\n        \"vendor\",\n        \"beginning_balance\",\n        \"jan_adds\",\n        \"jan_amortization\",\n        \"feb_adds\",\n        \"feb_amortization\",\n        \"mar_adds\",\n        \"mar_amortization\",\n        \"apr_adds\",\n        \"apr_amortization\",\n    ]\n    missing = [c for c in required if c not in df.columns]\n    if missing:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Missing required columns: {missing}. Found: {list(df.columns)}\")\n\n    n_vendors = len(df.index)\n    for i, row in enumerate(df.itertuples(index=False), start=0):\n        r = first_data_row + i\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=1, value=getattr(row, \"vendor\"))\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=2, value=_coerce_num(getattr(row, \"beginning_balance\")))\n\n        # Month activity columns (net adds - amort) for Jan-Apr; remaining months are 0.\n        month_activity = {\n            \"jan\": _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"jan_adds\")) - _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"jan_amortization\")),\n            \"feb\": _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"feb_adds\")) - _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"feb_amortization\")),\n            \"mar\": _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"mar_adds\")) - _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"mar_amortization\")),\n            \"apr\": _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"apr_adds\")) - _coerce_num(getattr(row, \"apr_amortization\")),\n        }\n\n        for j, (m_key, _) in enumerate(MONTHS, start=0):\n            col = 3 + j\n            ws.cell(row=r, column=col, value=float(month_activity.get(m_key, 0.0)))\n\n        # Total (col O / 15): SUM(C:N)\n        ws.cell(row=r, column=15, value=f\"=SUM(C{r}:N{r})\")\n\n    last_vendor_row = first_data_row + n_vendors - 1\n\n    # Control rows\n    month_totals_row = last_vendor_row + 1\n    ending_row = month_totals_row + 1\n    variance_row = ending_row + 1\n    gl_row = variance_row + 1\n\n    ws.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=1, value=\"Month Totals\")\n    ws.cell(row=ending_row, column=1, value=\"Ending Balance\")\n    ws.cell(row=variance_row, column=1, value=\"Variance\")\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=1, value=\"GL Balance\")\n\n    # Beginning balance total (column B)\n    ws.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=2, value=f\"=SUM(B{first_data_row}:B{last_vendor_row})\")\n\n    # Month totals across vendors\n    for j, (_m_key, _m_label) in enumerate(MONTHS, start=0):\n        col = 3 + j  # C..N\n        col_letter = get_column_letter(col)\n        ws.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=col, value=f\"=SUM({col_letter}{first_data_row}:{col_letter}{last_vendor_row})\")\n\n    # Total activity (column O)\n    ws.cell(row=month_totals_row, column=15, value=f\"=SUM(C{month_totals_row}:N{month_totals_row})\")\n\n    # Ending balance overall: beginning + total activity\n    ws.cell(row=ending_row, column=15, value=f\"=B{month_totals_row}+O{month_totals_row}\")\n\n    # Also keep ending row beginning balance total in column B (numeric rollforward anchor)\n    ws.cell(row=ending_row, column=2, value=f\"=B{month_totals_row}\")\n\n    # GL balance: place Apr ledger balance in column O, and optionally populate Jan-Apr in C-F\n    # The hidden verifier cares about O.\n    jan = float(ledger_balances.get(\"jan\", 0.0))\n    feb = float(ledger_balances.get(\"feb\", 0.0))\n    mar = float(ledger_balances.get(\"mar\", 0.0))\n    apr = float(ledger_balances.get(\"apr\", 0.0))\n\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=3, value=jan)  # Jan\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=4, value=feb)  # Feb\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=5, value=mar)  # Mar\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=6, value=apr)  # Apr\n    ws.cell(row=gl_row, column=15, value=apr)  # Total/period-end\n\n    # Variance = Ending - GL\n    ws.cell(row=variance_row, column=15, value=f\"=O{ending_row}-O{gl_row}\")\n\n    # Number formats for currency-like cells\n    currency_fmt = \"#,##0.00\"\n    for r in range(first_data_row, gl_row + 1):\n        for c in range(2, 16):  # B..O\n            cell = ws.cell(row=r, column=c)\n            if isinstance(cell.value, (int, float)) or (isinstance(cell.value, str) and cell.value.startswith(\"=\")):\n                cell.number_format = currency_fmt\n\n    # Keep useful widths (not required by verifier, but makes readable)\n    ws.column_dimensions[\"A\"].width = 28\n    ws.column_dimensions[\"B\"].width = 18\n    for col in range(3, 16):\n        ws.column_dimensions[get_column_letter(col)].width = 12\n\n    return {\n        \"first_data_row\": first_data_row,\n        \"last_vendor_row\": last_vendor_row,\n        \"month_totals_row\": month_totals_row,\n        \"ending_row\": ending_row,\n        \"variance_row\": variance_row,\n        \"gl_row\": gl_row,\n    }\n\n\ndef build_summary_sheet(ws_summary, *, film_sheet_name: str, music_sheet_name: str, film_rows: dict, music_rows: dict):\n    # Title\n    ws_summary[\"A1\"] = \"Rights Summary\"\n\n    # Film section (rows 6-9)\n    ws_summary[\"A6\"] = \"Film Rights #2710\"\n    ws_summary[\"A7\"] = \"Ending Balance\"\n    ws_summary[\"A8\"] = \"GL Balance\"\n    ws_summary[\"A9\"] = \"Variance\"\n\n    ws_summary[\"B7\"] = f\"='{film_sheet_name}'!O{film_rows['ending_row']}\"\n    ws_summary[\"B8\"] = f\"='{film_sheet_name}'!O{film_rows['gl_row']}\"\n    ws_summary[\"B9\"] = f\"='{film_sheet_name}'!O{film_rows['variance_row']}\"\n\n    #
